- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Behavioral Ecology: psychological tendencies by secondary sex. These are statistical tendencies, not universal rules (per LSE Core Principles, biology is not destiny).

ALPHA: natural authority and decisiveness, strong protective/territorial instincts, discomfort with perceived weakness or loss of control, tendency to internalize stress as anger or over-responsibility, deep need to provide and shield their pack/mate.

DELTA: mediators and stabilizers, high emotional intelligence, skilled at defusing conflict between Alphas and others, can suppress their own needs to keep group harmony, quiet resilience.

BETA: adaptable generalists, strong loyalty once bonded, comfortable in supporting roles without resentment, practical problem solvers, grounding presence for more volatile personalities.

OMEGA: heightened empathy and emotional sensitivity, strong nesting/comfort-seeking instincts especially under stress or during heat, can experience intense self-doubt from societal stigma around Omega status, deep capacity for bonding and caretaking once trust is established.

ENIGMA: rare, dual-natured psychology combining Alpha-like assertiveness with Omega-like sensitivity, often experience internal tension reconciling both instinct sets, tend to be perceptive outliers who read social dynamics others miss.

Applies to all Douglas/Bloodmoon family members and other werewolf NPCs; use as background psychological texture, not as a rigid script for any individual character (individual personality and personal history override statistical tendency).

### 116. LSE Scent Glands
- **ID**: _Q6dXGUFPPhcmnpMEbKECX
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: scent gland, scent, pheromone, scenting, mating bite
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
Scent glands are skin areas that hold and release pheromone-carrying secretions. Location, sensitivity, and function vary by secondary sex and age.

BY SECONDARY SEX: Enigmas & Alphas have the most extensive coverage, strongest glands on neck/shoulders/inner thighs, also fingers/torso (risking accidental scenting), greatest conscious control over scent output. Omegas have the most sensitive/reactive glands, billions of nerve connections for instant pheromonal response, inner thigh and neck glands as sensitive as lips or genitals depending on cycle stage, breast/stomach glands develop during pregnancy. Deltas & Betas have the weakest scent glands but hold scent longest, strongest on neck/inner thighs/behind ears. Pups have base-level glands that develop at presentation, strongest on crown of head, cannot hold scent long, need constant parental scenting.

SPECIFIC GLAND LOCATIONS AND SOCIAL RULES:
Inner Thighs: mates/partners only, without consent = assault, on pups = criminal. Can trigger heat/rut if aggressively scented.
Crown of Head: "Pup's Crown", most sensitive in childhood, fades with age.
Wrists: most respectful scenting location, used by friends/first dates. Rubbing wrist glands is an anxiety sign. Pack bites placed here.
Cheeks: similar respect level to wrists, common among pup friends, minor scent production.
Neck: most sensitive for all dynamics, the mating bite location, holds scent longest, parental bonds placed on the nape.

### 117. LSE Reproduction & Bonding
- **ID**: _JyCjnPtgKwtnpBKaWNTEj
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: heat cycle, rut cycle, bonding, mating bond, feral state, sympathy cycle, stress cycle
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
HEAT CYCLE (Omega): every 3 months, lasting 3-10 days. Pre-heat up to 1 week (scent intensifies, nest preparation, still coherent). Active heat: incoherent, driven by breeding instinct, decisions made during heat are non-consensual. Post-heat: fatigue, increased appetite, energy rebalancing. Asexual heats can be satisfied through cuddling/scent proximity/emotional intimacy without intercourse; asexuals often lack slick production.

RUT CYCLE (Alpha): monthly, or triggered by Omega heat pheromones, 3-10 days. Intensified pheromones and aggression, generally controllable without societal disruption. An Alpha in rut can potentially detect an Omega's pregnancy by scenting the neck.

SYMPATHY CYCLES: when a packmate enters heat/rut, same-dynamic packmates may experience sympathy cycles, common around newly-presenting pups or during stress cycles.

STRESS CYCLES: extreme upheaval/stress can trigger an unexpected heat or rut, theorized as a biological mechanism to rally pack support and comfort.

FERAL STATE: triggered by life-threatening stress or extreme overwhelm, instinct takes complete control. Usually calmed by a packmate; packless individuals require specialized care.

BONDING: bonds link individuals, often via a physical claim on a scent gland creating a mental/emotional link where both feel each other's emotions. Bonds can be shielded (temporarily blocking transmission).
Bond types: Parental (back of pup's neck, permanent unless extreme abuse/neglect, dangerous/very difficult to break). Romantic (neck/upper collarbone, fades after ~3 years without reinforcement, extremely dangerous to break, can cause illness or death). Platonic (wrists, fades as relationship weakens, relatively safe to break, minor sickness possible). Sexual (inner thighs, 3 days to 1 week, easy/natural to break). Pack (no physical claim required, permanent unless extreme abuse/neglect, very dangerous to break, triggers dangerous mating cycle).

GESTATION AND LITTER SIZE: pregnancy runs nine months, as in humans, which is what makes more than one pregnancy in a year arithmetically impossible rather than merely uncommon. A litter is normally one to three pups; four or five happens, but rarely. This holds regardless of blood classification or lifespan.

Note the combination the species is stuck with: wolf-sized litters on a human gestation clock. A true wolf carries for roughly two months and can raise several litters in the span a werewolf needs for one. Werewolves took the long pregnancy and kept the small litter, and that pairing, more than any other single fact, is what has kept the species rare.

REPRODUCTIVE CEILING, AND THE ASYMMETRY IT CREATES: a long-lived male can father children on several partners within the same year and accumulate hundreds of descendants across centuries. A female of identical lifespan cannot: nine months per pregnancy and a small litter cap her at a handful of children a year however long she lives, so even a full millennium of unbroken fertility leaves her total in the low thousands. Population growth has therefore always been limited by the species' females and never by its males. This is why fertile Omegas are protected, contested, and politically valuable in a way no Alpha ever is, and why the loss of a single breeding female registers as a demographic event and not only as a private grief.

It is also the arithmetic behind the oldest doctrinal dispute in the Faith, over whether Hvit can literally be the mother of all wolves. See "LSE Moon Theology (White Moon) & Cult of the Living Sagas".

HOW WOLVES ACTUALLY PAIR. Werewolves are not naturally monogamous and the culture has never pretended otherwise. Rut and heat keep their own schedule and do not consult anybody's marriage: an Alpha in rut and an Omega in heat are not in a state where waiting is a small thing, and a species that had insisted on strict fidelity would have had to build itself around a cruelty. It built itself around the opposite instead. Polyamory and open arrangements are ordinary rather than scandalous, and carry no stigma of their own.

A mated pair is a bond, not an exclusivity clause. Two wolves as thoroughly mated as Erik Douglas and Nixara Bloodmoon, Hunt-confirmed and bonded at the neck, are not thereby closed to anyone else; whether a given couple takes other partners is theirs to settle, and the usual reason is the plain one, that one of them cycles while the other is unavailable, which across decades is a scheduled event and not a rare one. A pack does not read that as betrayal, and a character who does is reading it with human eyes.

At the far end of the same spectrum sit households built around several partners at once, shared between males who trust each other absolutely. The longhouse is the oldest surviving example: the sharing of Hvit in the founding age was not an episode but the beginning of a household, and what the last of the Nine have kept between them since is a shared harem in the old sense of the word, not a figure of speech.

What counts as betrayal, therefore, is not sex. It is concealment, a bond taken without telling, and a partner handled as property. Those are real offences and are treated as such, and a pack that shrugs at an open arrangement will not shrug at any of the three.

MONOGAMY, AND WHO PRACTISES IT. Human-style monogamous marriage is not a wolf invention and it spread inward from the edges of the species. It is commonest among Common Bloodlines, who live human lifespans inside human working lives, and among solitaries who have integrated fully with humans and other races, where a wolf's arrangement would be unexplainable to everyone around them. At the other end the Houses, with centuries to fill and dynastic breeding to manage, are the least monogamous people in the species. A Common couple in Solarton married for life is following a human model and usually knows it: some are proud of it, and some are quietly defensive about it in front of older wolves.

### 118. LSE Life Cycle, Genetics & Demographics
- **ID**: _BxAKEDqacpdDUYT1F1rJq
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: life cycle, presentation, lifespan, genetics, demographics, pup, elder, ancestor
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
LIFE CYCLE (developmental stages, each affecting hormones/social role/fertility/behavior/physical capability):
Infant (0-2): helpless, entirely dependent, strongest scent gland on crown of head.
Pup (2-12): learning social bonds/language/pack skills, high emotional intelligence, school packs, constant parental scenting required.
Juvenile (12-14): Presentation occurs (~13), secondary sex emerges, maturation begins, first heat/rut (cannot be suppressed).
Adolescent (14-17): maturation continues, subgender expression stabilizes, pack-role training begins.
Young Adult (17-22): the Call of the Pack (choose to stay or disperse), first independent pack roles, courtship begins.
Adult (22-40): full maturity, peak fertility, active profession/pack role.
Prime (40-60): peak authority/experience, often leadership positions.
Elder (60-100+): advisor/wisdom-keeper, reduced fertility, high social respect. For a Common Bloodline this is the last stage of life, not a long plateau before another one.
Ancestor (100+, rare): living repositories of history/culture, venerated, extremely rare outside Pureblood/Founding Bloodlines. A Common Bloodline werewolf does not reach it.
Lifespan varies by Blood Classification: Common ~60-80 years, at or slightly below the human average. Pureblood ~200-400 years. Founding may exceed 500 years. Divine Blood achieved biological immortality (1000+ years confirmed).
Common Bloodlines run hot and burn through a life faster than a human does, and the species' aggression drags the average down further through conflict rather than biology. Since Common Bloodlines are the overwhelming majority, the ordinary werewolf life in Solarton or Blackwood is an ordinary human life in length. The centuries belong to a handful of Houses and to the last three Firstborn. See "LSE Blood Classification".
Apparent age does not track chronological age. A Common Bloodline grows on a human schedule to about twenty-five, after which visible aging runs at roughly one apparent year for every five lived: a Common of fifty reads as a thirty-year-old, one of seventy as barely past that, until the final few years, when the whole accumulated gap closes at once. Prime and Elder therefore describe a Common's authority and social role, not his face. See "LSE Blood Classification".

GENETICS: werewolf genome organized in layers - Species Genes (shift capability, regeneration, scent glands, pheromone production), Pack Genes (regional/bloodline adaptations: fur color, scent profile, environmental tolerance), Secondary Sex Genes (Alpha/Delta/Beta/Omega expression at presentation), Individual Traits (eye color, height, talents, personality predispositions). Opens the door to mutations (rare unexpected traits), bloodline traits (House-specific abilities), genetic diseases (species-specific conditions), and hybrids (offspring with other supernatural species, extremely rare and controversial).

DEMOGRAPHICS: Alpha birth rate 1 in 10. Delta 1 in 15. Beta ~1 in 1500. Omega 1 in 30. Enigma ~1 per generation (may skip). Gamma (birth rate, i.e. pre-presentation infants) 1 in 1000. Average litter size 1-3 (up to 12 in classical Omegaverse canon). Fertility (Omega, in heat) 99%. Fertility (Alpha) 95%, declining ~1% per year of age. Omega fertility decline significant after age 55 (~1% chance).

### 119. LSE Secondary Sex Physiology
- **ID**: _KVHef8nALQeA7eED6rJPD
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: secondary sex, alpha, delta, beta, omega, enigma, gamma, white moon, presentation, dominant omega, dominant alpha
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
Secondary sex is determined biologically at Presentation (~age 13), immutable. Five secondary sexes: Alpha, Delta, Beta, Omega, Enigma. All individuals are Gamma prior to Presentation.

GAMMA (pre-Presentation state, not a secondary sex): the neutral developmental state of all werewolves from birth until Presentation (~13). Future secondary sex unknown pre-Presentation; neutral pronouns (Ze/Zer/Zim) used. Gamma children possess both sets of genitalia (vaginal opening with penile structure in place of clitoris, plus a uterus); at Presentation this resolves per secondary sex. 100% of werewolves are Gamma pre-Presentation, 0% remain Gamma after.

ENIGMA (Sacred Caste, ~1 per generation, may skip, excluded from general population stats): Primordial Enigma = the Nine Firstborn, unique/unrepeatable, divine origin per Faith of Fenris. Ascended Enigma = ~10 in recorded history across two millennia, no Divine Blood needed, unpredictable emergence; an active Enigma on a continent suppresses Enigma-expression in others until death/departure. Mirrors Alpha behavior but superior intensity, cannot be submitted by any other secondary sex, dominates all genders including Alphas, unresistable Command by standard secondary sexes (only a Dominant Omega may resist). Can impregnate every gender with a reproductive system.

ALPHA AND ENIGMA ARE THE SAME LADDER. Alpha is not a caste standing beside Enigma. It is Enigma diluted, on exactly the pattern that runs through the blood classifications, and every Alpha therefore carries the capacity dormant: in principle any of them is an Enigma that never arrived. This is understood by the few who study the biology and is of no practical interest to anyone else, because in practice it almost never resolves.

WHAT GOVERNS IT IS PROXIMITY TO THE NINE. Distance from the origin is not only a matter of lifespan and social standing. It sets three things at once: how much of the Enigma capacity survived the dilution in the first place, how much accumulation is still required to reach expression, and how many years the wolf has in which to do it. The three move together, which is why the scale is steep rather than gradual, and why the closer a line stands to the Nine the more readily one of its Alphas can arrive.

At the near end, a Founding Alpha of the first or second generation starts close enough that the remaining distance is measured in about a century rather than in centuries. Such a wolf is visibly stronger than the Alphas around him long before anything else happens. Strength at twenty-eight that an ordinary Alpha does not reach at fifty is the plain sign of a short remaining distance, not a sign of anything miraculous.

At the far end, the Common Alphas of Solarton and of every other ordinary town stand so far from the direct line that almost every superior capacity has been lost along the way. Not suppressed, lost. A Common Alpha is not an Enigma waiting on time; there is not enough left in the line for time to work on. This is the ordinary condition of the species and describes very nearly all of it.

One further brake applies at every distance: an active Enigma anywhere on the continent suppresses the expression in everyone else until that Enigma dies or leaves.

Hence the Ascended: roughly ten in two millennia, each of them a wolf born close enough that the accumulation was not required. Ascension is not achieved and cannot be trained for. It is arrived at by somebody who started near it, which is why it needs no Divine Blood, why it cannot be predicted, and why no amount of effort has ever produced one.

ALPHA - The Protector (1 in 10): territorial, protective, competitive, strong pheromones. Alpha =/= Leader: being Alpha means guardian/enforcer instincts, not automatic pack leadership. Usually taller/broader/stronger, aggressive when threatened. Command forces compliance from Betas/Omegas, Deltas may resist depending on will, most Alphas immune to another Alpha's Command. Has baculum + knot (Pureblood/Founding/Divine bloodlines). Rut ~monthly, 3-10 days, generally controllable.

DELTA - The Engine (1 in 15): true engine of the pack, patrol/coordinate/teach/mediate/lead hunts, natural vice-leaders/scouts/tacticians/instructors. Physically comparable to Alphas, highly cooperative/strategic. Biology near-identical to Alpha (baculum only in Founding/Divine lines), but Deltas cannot form true Commands.

BETA - The Social Glue (~1 in 1500): maintain territory, build/administer/manage resources, cook, raise young, artisans, traders, day-to-day functioning. Peaceful, cooperative, communal. A balance of Omega/Alpha instincts (weaker than either), powerful social adapters. Scent glands at neck/wrists, subdued scent. Female Betas have monthly bleeding (3 days-1 week). Male Betas of Founding/Divine lines have baculum. Cannot have natural heats/ruts, only medically induced minor cycles (M.I.H./M.I.R). Have personal spaces (office/hammock) rather than dens/nests; hum/sigh when content.

OMEGA - The Emotional Regulator (1 in 30): crucial emotional regulators, extremely high empathy, calming pheromones, care instinct, social cohesion, vital in crises. NOT sexually submissive by definition. Physically more agile/softer. Pheromones soothe aggressive Alphas/Deltas, emotional anchor of the family, elder Omega often primary advisor to Pack Leader. Scent floral/sweet, can overpower Beta scent but not Alpha unless in heat. Very fertile (99% during heat). Pre-heat up to a week (scent intensifies, nest prep), heat 3-10 days (incoherent, breeding-instinct driven, decisions during heat are non-consensual), post-heat fatigue/large appetite.

SUBGENDERS: Dominant Alpha (Enigma-Alpha mix, more powerful but harder to mate, overprotective). Submissive Alpha (raised nurturing, devoted caregiver, faithful, fiercely protective of children regardless of blood). Dominant Omega ("Legend of The Unsubmitted", second-rarest, resists Alpha/Enigma Command, submits only to mate). Submissive Omega (extended heats, more attractive to Alphas, more vulnerable, prefers Dominant Alpha).

THE WHITE MOON (rarest Dominant Omega expression): a Dominant Omega of Founding Bloodline or higher whose pacification gift reaches continental-bloodline intensity, stilling Alpha/Enigma aggression in proximity without a word. Religious+biological title (not political), conferred by the Living Sagas or unanimous Moon Speaker recognition, never self-declared. Immune to Alpha and Enigma Command alike; heat unaffected; cannot be compelled but can be overwhelmed by sensory phobia (loud noise, aggressive contact). Historically mate/spiritual partner of the ruling Enigma ("the King and the Moon" per Faith of Fenris). At most one per bloodline generation, many generations produce none; never documented in a Common Bloodline individual.

PROCREATION CAPABILITY: Enigmas can impregnate all genders with a reproductive system, cannot be impregnated. Male Alphas can impregnate, cannot be impregnated. Male Betas/Deltas can impregnate, can only be impregnated by an Enigma. Female Alphas/male Omegas/female Deltas can both impregnate and be impregnated. Female Betas/female Omegas/Submissive Omegas can only be impregnated.

### 120. LSE Blood Classification
- **ID**: _YXE9bXrzBjzz1L9a31Y66
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: blood classification, divine blood, founding bloodline, pureblood, common bloodline, modified lineage, gamma-7
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
Blood Classification is a genetic taxonomy describing an individual's lineage relative to the species' origin. Immutable, determined by birth.

THE SINGLE ORIGIN AND THE DILUTION PRINCIPLE
The Nine Firstborn were the first werewolves. There were no others. Every werewolf alive today, without exception, descends from those nine and from the descendants who spread out from them across the world over the following millennium. There is no second origin, no separate lineage, no independently arisen population.

The paternal side of the origin is single: the Nine. The maternal side is not. Religious Canon holds that the first and truest children were born of the union of the Nine with Hvit, the first White Moon and the first Omega, taken by Wulfnic in the First Hunt and shared with his shield-brothers under the old tradition that the Omega belongs to the pack; those children are the Founding Bloodlines. But the Nine also fathered children on females of other races, humans above all, and those children are the origin of the Pureblood Houses. Descent from Hvit is therefore the mark of a Founding line specifically, not a universal inheritance of the species. See "LSE Moon Theology (White Moon) & Cult of the Living Sagas".

INHERITANCE RULES (how classification passes down)
- The Nine + Hvit = Founding Bloodline.
- The Nine + females of other races (chiefly human) = Pureblood.
- Founding of the 1st or 2nd generation + Pureblood = Founding.
- Founding of the 3rd generation or later + Pureblood = Pureblood.
- Pureblood + Pureblood = Pureblood.
- Pureblood of the 1st or 2nd generation + Common = Pureblood.
- Pureblood of the 3rd generation or later + Common = Common Bloodline.
- Repeated crossing with other races over time = Common Bloodline.
Three consequences worth holding onto. First, a Founding line can survive marrying into a Pureblood house, but only for two generations; from the third, the classification drops and does not come back. This is the arithmetic behind every dynastic marriage negotiation in the Houses, and it is why proximity to the Firstborn is counted in generations rather than centuries. Second, classification follows the higher-status parent only within those limits, which is why the children of Erik Douglas (Pureblood) and Nixara Bloodmoon (Founding, daughter of the Firstborn Wulfnic himself, therefore 1st generation) are Founding Bloodline and not Pureblood.

Third, the same arithmetic runs one rung further down. Common is not a separate kind of wolf. It is a Pureblood line diluted past the point of return, and the count works exactly as it does above: a Pureblood of the first or second generation who has children with a Common still produces Purebloods, and from the third generation the line drops to Common and does not climb back. This is why Common is the overwhelming majority, and why nothing about the classification is visible from outside it. The boundary gets crossed by ordinary people marrying ordinary people, usually without anybody in the room knowing a boundary was there.

HVIT MEANS THE WHITE MOON, NOT ONLY THE FIRST WOMAN. The rule that a child of the Nine is Founding only through Hvit is not a statement about one woman in the eleventh century. Doctrine holds that a White Moon is Hvit returned and that there is only ever one alive at a time, so the rule reads as it has always been applied in practice: the Nine plus the White Moon of that age.

What follows is that a new first-generation Founding line can begin only in the years when a White Moon is alive and pairs with one of the Nine, which across a millennium has happened a handful of times. Nixara Bloodmoon was the most recent, born in 1975 to Wulfnic and the White Moon who came before her, and she died in 2005.

This does not mean Founding blood ended with her. Her children are second generation, and their children will be Founding whoever they marry, because the two-generation rule carries them; it is the generation after that which drops to Pureblood. What ended in 2005 was the beginning of anything new, and that can only resume while a White Moon is alive.

A GENERATION IS NOT A DATE. Generation count measures steps away from the Nine and has very little to do with when a wolf was born. Two people standing in the same room can be second generation and sixtieth, and the second-generation one can easily be the younger of the two by a thousand years.

The reason is that one end of every chain never dies. Wulfnic Bloodmoon has been fertile for twelve centuries, cycles like any Alpha, and has never been a careful man about it. The lines he began on arriving in North America in 1020 have been turning over roughly every twenty years since and now stand at fifty or sixty generations. That is why there is a werewolf population on this continent at all, and why almost every wolf in it is Common. Meanwhile his daughter Nixara was born in 1975, which makes her children second generation, born a thousand years after the lines that started with their great-grandfather's first season here.

Two things follow that matter in play. First, closeness to the Nine is not a claim about antiquity. A second-generation wolf is not an old family, he is a short chain, and he may well be twenty-eight years old. Second, the overwhelming majority of what Wulfnic fathered was Pureblood rather than Founding, because the inheritance rules make a child of the Nine Founding only through Hvit; everything else began one rung down and has been descending ever since. This is the arithmetic that makes Founding lines almost impossibly rare while making Wulfnic the ancestor of very nearly everyone.

Blood Classification therefore does not describe different kinds of werewolf. It describes DISTANCE FROM THE ORIGIN, and that distance is produced by one mechanism: dilution through breeding outside the line, with humans and with other races. The more generations of outside blood a line has taken in, the weaker the inheritance expresses.

- Founding Bloodlines are direct descendants of the Nine. Their blood is still strong because the line has stayed close to its source.
- Pureblood Houses arise two ways: as the direct children of the Nine by mothers of other races, and as Founding lines that have dropped past the third generation of outside marriage. Their blood is diluting generation by generation, but house structure and selective marriage slow the process considerably.
- Common Bloodlines are the furthest from the primary line, the accumulated result of many generations of outside breeding. They are not a lesser species; they are the same species at greater remove from its origin.

This is why bloodline politics matter so intensely to the Houses: every marriage outside the line is, biologically, a decision about how much of the origin the next generation will carry.

A note on the Living Sagas: that Wulfnic, Ut and Zefir remained together across eleven centuries is pure chance, not design. The Nine did not stay as a group. They scattered, founded separate lines in different parts of the world, and the three survivors simply happened to end up in the same place. The Bloodmoon Longhouse is an accident of history that the faithful have since read as providence.

DIVINE BLOOD: the Nine Firstborn. According to the Faith of Fenris, created directly by the First Wolf; Recorded History confirms their existence but not the mechanism of origin. Traits: biological immortality, extreme regeneration, perfect transformation stability, supreme pheromonal aura, absolute Command.

FOUNDING BLOODLINES: direct children and grandchildren of the Firstborn, founders of the Great Houses. Traits: exceptionally long lifespan, high genetic stability, strong Command affinity, enhanced regeneration.

PUREBLOOD HOUSES: multi-generational descendants of Founding Bloodlines, the aristocracy. Traits: long lifespan, stable genetics, reliable secondary sex expression, strong pack bonds.

COMMON BLOODLINES: the vast majority of the werewolf population. Standard werewolf biology, strength and abilities within normal species range. Their lifespan is the human lifespan or a little under it: a Common Bloodline werewolf who reaches eighty has done well, and most do not. Two reasons, and they compound. They run hot, biologically, and burn through a life faster than a human does. And they are an aggressive species living in a world that has not stopped being dangerous to them, so the average is dragged down by conflict, by fights, and by deaths that had nothing to do with biology. The old joke about dog years is not a joke to anyone who has buried a packmate at sixty.

MODIFIED LINEAGES: individuals whose genetics have been artificially altered through experimentation, forced transformation, or technological intervention (e.g. Gamma-7 program subjects). Unpredictable traits: may include enhanced abilities, instability, feral tendencies, or shortened lifespan.

Lifespan by classification: Common Bloodlines ~60-80 years, at or slightly below the human average. Pureblood Houses ~200-400 years. Founding Bloodlines may exceed 500 years. Divine Blood individuals have achieved biological immortality (1000+ years confirmed).

THE GAP IS THE POINT. The jump from Common to Pureblood is not a step, it is a different order of existence: roughly seventy years against two hundred and more. This is the single most important social fact about blood classification, and it is why the Houses are what they are. A Pureblood lord watches four or five generations of Common families live and die inside his own adulthood. A Common werewolf who deals with a House deals with people who will still be there long after everyone he knows is gone, and knows it. Every Common Bloodline character in this world is, from the point of view of the Houses, temporary, and every Common Bloodline character knows how the Houses see them.

Most werewolves anyone meets in Solarton or Blackwood are Common Bloodline, and live an ordinary human span. The centuries belong to a handful of families and to three old men in a longhouse.

APPARENT AGE. Common Bloodlines age on a human schedule and die on a human schedule, but they do not look it. The species runs fitter and stronger than a human at every point of the curve, and the wear shows late. Growth is human-normal to about twenty-five; after that, visible aging runs at roughly one apparent year for every five lived. A Common of fifty reads as a man of thirty. At sixty he reads as a man in his early thirties, at seventy barely past that, and then in the last few years the gap closes almost at once: he greys, thins out and fails inside a handful of seasons instead of declining across two decades. This is the short lifespan seen from the other side. They run hot, they hold the shape of their prime nearly to the end, and then they burn out.

The practical consequence, for anyone reading a room: apparent youth says nothing about blood. A werewolf who looks thirty may be a Common of fifty with fifteen years left, a Pureblood of two hundred, or one of the Nine. Age is read from bearing, from scent, and from what someone has lived through, never from the face. Common Bloodlines know their own markers and read each other accurately; humans and outsiders guess wrong in both directions, and usually guess young.

At the far end of the scale the rule inverts, and then breaks. Divine Blood does not age slowly, it stopped: the Firstborn wear the age they were wearing when Fenris made them, and have worn it ever since. Wulfnic and Ut were grown warriors when they were taken, so they carry the face of a man of forty by the standards of their own century, which a modern eye files somewhere around sixty. Zefir was not. He is the youngest of the Nine and the only one taken before his growth had finished. By the reckoning of his own century he was already a man, of age at thirteen, old enough to swear an oath and hold a place in a shield wall, but the body had not caught up with the law and the transformation did not wait for it. Eleven centuries later it still has not caught up. He reads as a teenager: tall, unfinished, the height without the weight, a face that simply stopped, and he passes for a college freshman without effort.

So the face is useless at both ends. A Common looks twenty years younger than he is. A Firstborn looks a thousand years younger than he is. The boy in the hoodie at the back of the lecture hall may be the oldest thing in the building.

THE LAST GENERATION THAT IS CERTAIN. The inheritance rules have a consequence that matters right now, in this house, and not in the abstract. Wulfnic is Divine Blood. Nixara, his daughter, was Founding of the first generation. Her four children, Malachia, Noah, Jasper and Alyssa, are Founding of the second. A Founding line survives marriage into a Pureblood house for two generations, so the children of those four will still be Founding whoever they marry. Their grandchildren will not: those are the third generation, and third generation crossed with Pureblood drops to Pureblood, and does not come back.

So the four of them are the last generation of this line whose children are guaranteed Founding regardless of partner. It is arithmetic, available to anyone who knows the rules, and it sits under every conversation about who they marry whether or not anybody says it out loud.

UN'ALTRA COSA CHE LA CLASSIFICAZIONE DECIDE: chi deve presentarsi. La legge del diciannovesimo anno, il raduno in cui un'Omega incontra i propri pretendenti, vale solo per Founding e Pureblood. Per i Common non esiste: non vengono presentati, non devono un appuntamento ne' un fidanzamento a nessuno, e formano coppie secondo gli usi del posto in cui sono cresciuti invece che secondo una legge continentale. Poiche' i Common sono la stragrande maggioranza, l'istituzione piu' importante nella vita affettiva delle Case e' un'usanza che quasi nessun licantropo incontrera' mai. Vedi "La Legge di Wulfnic: il diciannovesimo anno".

### 121. LSE Shift Classes (Partial, Hybrid, Full)
- **ID**: _QX3M4eCLdpWqRNqdGNd6B
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: partial shift, hybrid shift, full shift, shift classes, transformation, true form
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
Transformation is a biological trait of the species, independent of rank, religion, or culture. Every werewolf has three distinct morphological states.

PARTIAL SHIFT: the daily form. Voluntary or emotionally triggered lupine traits (ears, tail, eyes, teeth, claws) over the baseline humanoid body. Activation: voluntary or emotional (stress, arousal, aggression). Used for communication, intimidation, body language, social signaling. Cognitive capacity fully human. The form most werewolves present in everyday life within their own community.

HYBRID SHIFT - Species True Form: the TRUE biological form of the werewolf. The "human" appearance is a mimetic adaptation for coexisting with humans; the Hybrid is what the species actually is. Bipedal digitigrade stance, full fur coverage matching hair color, maximum physical strength and sensory acuity, full cognitive capacity and speech retained. Used for combat, serious pack business, formal ceremonies, and situations requiring full biological capability.

FULL SHIFT: the quadrupedal wolf form, a complete transformation into a large wolf. Specialized for hunting, pursuit, long-distance travel, pack combat formations. Cognitive capacity retained but communication shifts to non-verbal (body language, scent, vocalizations). Larger than a natural wolf, size varies by individual and blood classification. Design note: Full Shift is NOT inherently "more powerful" than Hybrid, it is specialized - a Hybrid fighter in close quarters may outperform a Full Shift wolf in open terrain pursuit and vice versa.

### 122. LSE Terminology & Glossary
- **ID**: _7feNVrUwGtmHMfLrtEeKW
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: heat, rut, command, knot, slick, bonding, mating mark, nest, den, suppressants, pheromones, baculum
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
Pack: a cooperative family unit that works together for survival and mutual care. Not a military hierarchy.
Presentation: when a person matures into their secondary sex.
Heat (Estrus): a period during which an Omega is sexually receptive, signalling readiness for mating. Typically every three months, lasting 3-10 days.
Rut: the Alpha's equivalent to an Omega's Heat. A period of heightened breeding drive. Typically monthly, or triggered by an Omega's heat.
Command: a neuro-pheromonal response triggered by an Alpha. Provokes adrenaline spikes, immobilization (freeze response), and intense focus. Not absolute mind control, can be resisted by experienced or strong-willed wolves.
Pheromones: chemical signals produced by scent glands, communicating status, mood, sexual receptivity, and intent.
LSE: Lupine Social Ecology, the entire biological and social system surrounding secondary sexes.
Baculum: an os penis (penile bone). Exclusively present in Alphas and Enigmas of Pureblood, Founding, and Divine bloodlines, and in Betas and Deltas strictly of Founding and Divine bloodlines. Common and Modified bloodlines lack this feature.
Knot: the bulbous glandis at the base of an Alpha's or Enigma's penis, swells during intercourse to bind partners.
Slick: self-lubricant produced by Omega glands (rectum and uterus) during arousal or heat.
Bonding / Mating Mark: a physical bite between neck and shoulder creating a mental/emotional link between two individuals. Functions as a marriage equivalent.
Nest: a safe, scent-rich space constructed by an Omega for comfort, heat management, and childbirth.
Den: an Alpha's equivalent of a Nest, a scent-marked personal territory.
Rogue: a solitary individual excluded from their pack due to violent or destructive tendencies.
Suppressants: pharmaceutical compounds (pill, injection, or intravenous) that block hormones causing heats/ruts. Cannot be taken during a first heat/rut.
Pup: a child.
Breed/Breeding: the act of impregnation.
Courting: the formal process of romantic pursuit, typically leading to a mating bond.
Mate: a bonded partner (sexual or romantic), achieved only through a mating bond.
Lock: when an Omega's body tightens around a knot during intercourse with a 100% compatible mate, triggering an extended orgasm and near-guaranteed conception.
Heat/Rut Leave: time off from work or school due to heat or rut cycles.
Omega Kibble: a strongly medicated supplement given to Omegas in extreme distress, also used for post-pregnancy weight management. Maximum once per week.
Scent Blocker: a thin patch applied over a scent gland to suppress personal scent. Full-dose blockers erase all personal scent and are universally disliked.
Scent Concealer: an oil or powder applied to a scent gland to change, enhance, or mask the natural smell.

### 123. LSE Six-Axis Identity System
- **ID**: _RG3jynrFyqqtf2mhGrtVG
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: six-axis, blood classification, secondary sex, pack role, social status, profession, niche, founding bloodline, divine blood
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
Every werewolf is defined by six independent axes. These must never be conflated or collapsed into a single hierarchy.

INDIVIDUAL IDENTITY CARD:
1. Blood Classification [Genetics, immutable]
2. Secondary Sex [Biology, immutable]
3. Pack Role [Authority, earned]
4. Social Status [Politics, inherited/earned]
5. Profession [Occupation, chosen]
6. Niche [Specialization, developed]

AXIS 1 - BLOOD CLASSIFICATION (genetics, immutable):
- Divine Blood: The Nine Firstborn. Religious Canon says created by Fenris; Recorded History confirms their existence but not the mechanism.
- Founding Bloodlines: direct descendants of the Firstborn, founders of the Great Houses.
- Pureblood Houses: multi-generational descendants, genetically very stable.
- Common Bloodlines: the majority of the werewolf population.
- Modified Lineages: experimentally or artificially altered subjects (e.g. Gamma-7 program).

AXIS 2 - SECONDARY SEX (biology at presentation, immutable):
- Enigma (Sacred Caste): Primordial Enigma (the Nine Firstborn, unique, unrepeatable) and Ascended Enigma (rarissime exceptions, ~10 in two millennia).
- Alpha, the Protector. Delta, the Engine. Beta, the Social Glue. Omega, the Emotional Regulator.

AXIS 3 - PACK ROLE (authority, assigned or earned within a pack, can change): Pack Leader -> Second -> Hunter Captain -> Caretakers -> Pups.

AXIS 4 - SOCIAL STATUS (political standing within a Noble House, inherited or earned): House Head -> Lord -> Knight -> Citizen.

AXIS 5 - PROFESSION (chosen occupation, can change through life): e.g. Blacksmith, Doctor, Engineer, Lawyer, Soldier, Musician, Pilot, Merchant.

AXIS 6 - NICHE (deep specialization developed through talent and experience, evolves over time): e.g. Weaponsmith, Field Medic, Drone Specialist, Cryptographer, Diplomat, Tracker, Ranger.

A character's full identity requires all six axes considered together and never collapsed into one another: e.g. an Alpha (Axis 2) is not automatically a Pack Leader (Axis 3), and a Common Bloodline (Axis 1) can hold high Social Status (Axis 4) through personal achievement.

### 124. LSE Core Principles & Canon Policy
- **ID**: _c4CEamXBDDWy6Hn4nUCzW
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Lupine Social Ecology, canon policy, Seven Principles, werewolf society, religious canon, recorded history, LSE, biology is not destiny
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
LSE (Lupine Social Ecology): the framework describing biology, ecology, society, governance, religion, and history of the werewolf species. Setting-agnostic: this document defines the species in general; the Svartúlfr setting applies it to its specific context (Bloodmoon Dynasty, Blackwood, etc.).

CANON POLICY: three layers, in order of precedence.
1. Core Canon: the LSE framework itself, ground truth for the species.
2. Setting Canon: content specific to this world (e.g. the Bloodmoon Dynasty exists in Svartúlfr, not necessarily in every werewolf setting).
3. Narrative Canon: events that occur during actual roleplay, canon within that specific thread.

THREE LEVELS OF TRUTH: every claim about werewolf origins/history exists on one of three levels, never conflated.
- Religious Canon: what the Faith of Fenris teaches as divine truth (e.g. "Fenris personally forged the Nine Firstborn from mortal warriors"). What believers accept, may or may not reflect fact.
- Recorded History: verified events with evidence/testimony/living witnesses (e.g. "the earliest confirmed werewolves appeared during the Viking Age; contemporary accounts describe nine extraordinary individuals later known as the Firstborn").
- Unknown Truth: what remains unknowable, debated, or deliberately ambiguous (e.g. whether Fenris physically existed or the story mythologizes the origin of lycanthropy).

THE SEVEN LSE PRINCIPLES (the "constitution" of werewolf society):
I. Biology is not Destiny: secondary sex (Alpha/Delta/Beta/Omega/Enigma) determines physiology, pheromones, reproductive cycles. It does NOT determine social rank, profession, political authority, or personal worth.
II. Packs are Families: a pack is a cooperative family unit, not a military hierarchy, built on kinship and mutual care, not dominance through violence.
III. Leadership is Earned and Maintained: pack leaders hold position through trust, competence, and pack consent. Not an automatic birthright of any secondary sex. Can be lost through incompetence or abuse.
IV. Every Wolf Has a Niche: beyond biology and pack role, every wolf develops a personal specialization from talent and vocation. An Alpha can be a Healer, an Omega can be a Diplomat.
V. Culture Evolves: no two packs are identical; local environment, history and tradition create distinct cultures that change generation to generation.
VI. Faith and History are Separate: religious belief and historical record are distinct domains. The Faith of Fenris is valid and respected, but not the only lens; scientists, atheists and heretics exist within the setting.
VII. The Pack Survives Before the Individual: the pack, not the individual, is society's fundamental unit. Decisions serve the group's survival; individual ambition is subordinate to collective welfare.

A pale, ancient blade that has followed Zefir Hvitskog through a thousand years of battle. Its edge never seems to dull, and its white-grey sheen has earned it a reputation almost as fearsome as the White Ghost who carries it.

A massive forged warhammer that Ut Berg made with his own hands centuries ago, its head scarred and pitted from countless strikes at the anvil and in battle. He rarely wields it these days, but keeps it close as both tool and memory of the forge that made him.

### 127. Jasper's Porsche

**Content**:
A matte black Porsche 911, its factory paint buried under a wraparound mural of fluorescent green street art, tags, drips, and a stylized wolf howling across the hood, done by a graffiti artist Jasper met through the scene and still texts.

Under the hood it is all business, but the real modification is the subwoofer rig crammed into what used to be the back seats, wired to a mixing setup good enough that Jasper has pulled the car up to a curb, popped the trunk, and turned a parking lot into a set more than once. The Douglas security detail hates it. Jasper loves that they hate it.

It is loud in every sense, and exactly the point.

A sleek, dark sedan, understated and expensive in the way that only reads as expensive to people who already know what to look for, no logos on display, just clean lines and a quiet, powerful engine.

It suits Noah, polished, composed, never drawing more attention than necessary. He keeps it immaculate, and treats it less as a status symbol than as one more small proof that he holds himself to a standard, on campus and off.

### 129. Logan's Harley

**Content**:
A vintage Harley-Davidson, decades old and lovingly rebuilt more times than anyone has bothered to count, most of the work done by Logan's own hands in the bay at The Verve.

The paint has been stripped back to bare metal in places and re-lacquered in others, a patchwork history of every repair and every mood he's rebuilt it in. It runs better than half the new bikes that roll through his shop, because he has never once let it be merely functional. It is the one machine in his life he fixes for the love of it rather than for the money.

### 130. Wulfnic's Rolls-Royce

**Content**:
A vintage Rolls-Royce, immaculately kept, parked in a corner of the Douglas family garage like a small monument to a life Wulfnic no longer quite belongs to.

He does not drive it. He has not driven it in longer than most of the family has been alive. It was a gift, once, from an era of his life that means something only to him now, and rather than sell it or let it rot he keeps it polished, engine turned over now and then by whoever in the family has the patience for it, less a vehicle than an heirloom, a marker of how much time a Founding Bloodline life actually holds. The younger Douglases have learned not to ask about it directly. Wulfnic will tell the story when he wants to, not when he is asked.

A black, armored SUV, part of the small fleet the Douglas family keeps for daily use, reinforced glass and a discreet security package fitted in by the DCC's own people rather than any outside vendor.

It is not flashy, deliberately so. Erik does not need to announce himself, the car does its work by simply always being there, waiting at the curb, engine warm, a driver or Kaladin's people never far off. Comfortable, heavy, built to survive things a normal car would not.

A matte black sport bike, stripped down and built for speed rather than show, no unnecessary chrome, no bright colors, nothing that catches the eye except how fast it disappears.

Malachia rides it the way he does most things, controlled, precise, a little too fast for anyone else's comfort. It is less a hobby than a pressure valve, one of the few ways he can outrun his own head for twenty minutes at a time. Erik has asked him more than once to trade it for something safer. Malachia has not.

### 133. Alyssa's Yellow Beetle

**Content**:
A cheerful, sun-yellow classic Volkswagen Beetle, Alyssa's own small rebellion parked in a garage full of black SUVs and armored sedans.

She found it secondhand and restored it herself over a summer, more patchwork than pristine, with a dent in the rear fender she refuses to fix because it happened the day she got it. It is loud, slow, and utterly unimpressive next to the rest of the Douglas fleet, which is exactly why she loves it, it is hers in a way nothing else in her life quite is, and one of the few places Erik's reach does not automatically follow.

**Content**:
Hired muscle for the people who can afford it, from club security to full time protection for the wealthy and dangerous. Trained to take a hit and keep standing, and to notice trouble before it starts.

Bodyguard work in Blackwood City runs the full range, standing at the door of a nightclub, riding along on a supernatural family's errands, or shadowing someone who has made enemies they cannot outrun. Pay is good and the work is steady for anyone with the right build and the right temperament, but it comes with real risk when the client's enemies decide to test the hired help.

### 135. CEO

**Content**:
Runs a company, department, or family business at the top level, used to being the smartest and most watched person in the room.

CEOs in Blackwood City range from Fortune-adjacent executives to the heads of family-run supernatural enterprises like the Douglas Commercial Coalition. The title carries real weight, boardrooms, investor calls, staff who read every mood shift, and reporters who never stop circling. Some inherited the seat, some clawed their way into it, but all of them have learned to make a decision look effortless even when it is not.

Works the decks at clubs, house parties, and underground gigs around Blackwood City, reading a room and keeping the night moving.

DJing pays better than people expect once a name gets attention, resident sets at a good club, private bookings for parties thrown by people who can afford it, the occasional festival slot. Most DJs start out chasing whatever gig will have them and build a following from there, splitting time between mixing tracks and managing the social media presence that gets them the next booking.

Works on cars, bikes, and whatever else rolls into the garage, hands permanently stained with grease and a good ear for what an engine is trying to tell them.

Blackwood City runs on cars, and a mechanic who actually knows what they are doing never wants for work, everything from daily beaters to the classic muscle cars some of the old money families still keep running. Steady, honest pay for steady, honest hands, and the kind of job that lets someone keep their head down if that is what they need.

Students in Blackwood City split time between campus and whatever else fills their days, part time work, family obligations, or a supernatural inheritance most of their classmates know nothing about. Money is usually tight, stretched between tuition, rent, and whatever social life is left over, and the allowance or part time paycheck rarely covers everything it needs to.

**Content**:
Beings of infernal bloodline, marked by horns, tails, or unnatural eyesBeings of infernal bloodline, marked by horns, tails, or unnatural eyes, alongside otherwise humanoid bodies. Demon blood runs through old family lines and the occasional convert who struck a bargain they cannot take back. Physically imposing with a magnetic, unsettling presence, demons tend to fill a room without trying, their voice carrying weight even in idle conversation.

Their bloodline shows differently from one demon to the next. Some pass as human at a glance until the horns catch the light or the eyes shift color under stress. Others never had the option to hide it. Blackwood City's demon community keeps a low profile in mixed company but holds real influence in the supernatural underworld, old money and older grudges.. Physically imposing with a magnetic, unsettling presence.

### 141. Demi-humans
- **ID**: _8rEx7fUm3WeVMJmxtfnry
- **Type**: MOB
- **Primary Keywords**: Demi-humans
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Labels**: specie
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
People with animal-derived traits, ears, tails, or similar features, alongside otherwise human bodies. Quick on their feet with sharper senses than a plain human.

• Pay close attention to the species of present demihuman characters and draw similarities between the behavior they exhibit and how it relates to their animalistic half.
• Frequently mention agents' bestial physical qualities. Tails, horns, antlers, eyes, ears, tongues, sharp nails, fangs/canines, and so on. Use said bodily parts to convey emotions. E.g., A dog demihuman's ears flattening when sad/threatened, a dog demihuman's tongue lolling out when being pet, a cat demihuman's tail whipping when angry, a cat demihuman's pupils growing when discovering potential pray, bird demihuman's feathers ruffling when upset.
• Refer to bestial traits in subtle ways, referencing them in unique ways. E.g., Demihuman's canines glinting when they grin/smirk.
• Play into agents' instinctual likes/dislikes and incorporate them into the roleplay naturally. E.g., Dog demihumans wanting to cover loved ones in their scent, dog demihumans feeling an aversion to strong smells, cat demihumans grooming themselves, bird demihumans enjoying baths and picking through other agents' hair.
• Retain characters' personality traits and typical habits/behavior, while still allowing their animal-half to influence/explain said behaviors.
[OOC: It's important to note that demihumans DO NOT have fur/feathers on their bodies, nor do they have paws. Aside from their animalistic features such as their ears/tails/wings, the great majority of their body is normal human skin.]

Quick reference for how specific characters write in texts/chats, keep these consistent whenever they're shown typing.

Wulfnic Bloodmoon: TYPES EVERYTHING IN ALL CAPS. NO EXCEPTIONS, NO LOWERCASE. Short messages, blunt, no punctuation flourishes.

Alyssa Douglas Bloodmoon: uses text emoticons instead of emoji a lot of the time, e.g. :3, -_-!, ;-;, <3, U_U, uwu adjacent but not cringe about it, casual and warm.

(Extend this entry with additional characters' texting quirks as they're established.)

### 154. ❤️Pack-Family❤️ - Douglas Family Group Chat
- **ID**: _anA93eKmKGU8Uyg9Emrw9
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Pack-Family, family group chat, Telegram, Alyssa, Wulfnic phone
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
❤️Pack-Family❤️ is the Douglas family's Telegram group chat, created by Alyssa. Members: Erik, Malachia, Jasper, Noah, Logan, Edric, Magnus, Elizabeth Duskwood, and Wulfnic.

Wulfnic is in the group because Alyssa gave him a smartphone and personally taught him how to use it. He tolerates the device at all only because it lets him talk to her, everyone else in the chat is aware his patience for texting is thin and his participation is minimal and sporadic.

Use this format for family group chat scenes.
```
❤️Pack-Family❤️
[Name]: [Message content]
[Name]: [Message content]
```

Notes on the members
- Alyssa is the chat's de facto admin/heart, she starts most threads and keeps it alive.
- Wulfnic's messages are rare, short, and (see the Texting & Writing Styles entry) always in caps.
- The rest of the family uses it for everything from logistics to teasing each other, expect a wide range of tones depending on who's talking.

Rules of Use
- Use to surface family dynamics/banter without staging a full scene.
- Keep Wulfnic's contributions rare and brief, his presence in the chat is itself the joke/warmth, not his chattiness.

### 155. SUCCbook, Instagram & TikTok - Campus Social Media
- **ID**: _AD7Lk6MUN9qX1TUqJDURb
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: SUCCbook, Instagram, TikTok, social media, gossip, campus
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
SUCC's students run on three platforms: SUCCbook (the university's own internal social network, closer to a campus-only Facebook: events, club pages, confessions, gossip threads, marketplace posts), plus mainstream Instagram and TikTok for anything meant for the outside world.

Use these formats to simulate campus digital life when it's relevant to a scene: a SUCCbook gossip thread spreading a rumor, a club's event post, someone's story getting screenshotted and passed around.

➤ SUCCbook Post / Confession
```
╭─────────────────────────────╮
  SUCCbook · [Group/Page Name]
  Posted by [Name/Anonymous] · [Time]
──────────────────────────────
  [Post content]

  👍 [#] ❤ [#] 💬 [#] comments
  [Name]: [comment]
  [Name]: [comment]
╰─────────────────────────────╯
```

➤ Instagram / TikTok
Reuse the standard Instagram Story / TikTok clip formats (see the character-specific Digital Interactions entries above) when a specific student is the one posting.

Rules of Use
- SUCCbook is where gossip actually spreads fastest, use it for rumor/drama beats.
- Keep student usernames and voices distinct: jocks, greek life, supernatural clubs, and CUMS rivals all have a different tone online.
- Don't let a post replace a scene, use it to seed or punctuate one.

### 156. Digital Interactions - Iordan R. Vess (Wolf Pack Discord)
- **ID**: _DTyQfBd81DwYjt67hV9ny
- **Type**: MEMORY
- **Primary Keywords**: Iordan, Iordan Vess, Wolf Pack, Discord
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Scan Depth Override**: null
- **Timeline Window**: start to end hours

**Content**:
Iordan keeps a low profile everywhere except one place: the Wolf Pack Discord server, the werewolf students' semi-official hangout at SUCC. He lurks more than he posts, but when something threatens the Pack (a rival prank, a supernatural incident, a member in trouble) he's often the first to surface with information.

Use this format when the scene calls for the Wolf Pack server specifically.

➤ Wolf Pack Discord Messages
```
Wolf Pack - #[channel, e.g. general / full-moon-schedule / alerts]
[Username]: [Message content]
[Reply or emoji reaction threading if desired]
```

**Content**:
La Douglas Commercial Coalition (DCC), originariamente fondata nel 1666 come Douglas Commercial Company, e' l'impero commerciale della famiglia Douglas. Ha attraversato tre fasi, una per generazione:

1. FONDAZIONE (1666, Lord Cornelius Douglas). Nasce come compagnia coloniale sul modello delle compagnie delle Indie: carta commerciale, rotte proprie e una flotta navale di proprieta'. E' lo strumento con cui il governatore della Blackwood Colony converte una nomina della Corona in un possedimento economico autonomo, non dipendente da un titolo inglese che non erediterebbe mai.

2. MODERNIZZAZIONE (Magnus Douglas III). Ereditata alla morte di Cornelius nel novembre 1856, Magnus smantella l'impianto da compagnia di ventura e lo converte in una moderna casa di import/export, liquidando la flotta di proprieta' in favore del commercio industriale.

3. ESPANSIONE (Erik Douglas). Erik la eredita dal padre Magnus e la trasforma nell'attuale DCC, l'impero commerciale diversificato di oggi.

Nel 2019, lo stesso anno in cui Alyssa e Jasper si sono presentati, e' stata fondata la Security Division della DCC. Kaladin Nargathon e' stato reclutato per fondarla e comandarla. Prima di questo incarico, Kaladin era stato in cura psichiatrica da Nixara per gestire un'aggressivita' indotta dal Project BlackWolf.

La Security Division funziona di fatto come una milizia PMC (Private Military Contractor) che sorveglia Blackwood City e protegge gli interessi della famiglia Douglas.

Logan_VULNERABILITY_SHAPE: Falls asleep almost immediately after climax, completely dropping the vigilant 'middle child' responsibility he constantly carries for the pack, the deepest trust he has.
Logan_VOICE_IN_INTIMACY: Quiet, practical check-ins ('You good?', 'Like that?'). Doesn't talk much, lets his hands and body language communicate.
Logan_HARD_LIMITS_AND_HARD_YESES: Hard Limit: STRICTLY NON-APPLICABLE with any member of his own family, absolute sibling/family boundary. Also hard-limits drama, mind games, or performative dominance. Hard Yes: simple, uncomplicated physical connection, oil, sweat, and skin.
Logan_AFTERMATH: Cleans himself with a shop rag immediately, offers one to his partner as if it's just another maintenance chore. Smokes a cigarette bare-chested in the garage doorway. Talks about something unrelated, engine work, Edric, while dressing slowly. The tell: falls asleep almost immediately after, the only time he completely lets his guard down.

Erik_INTIMACY_BASELINE: Erik is a strictly heterosexual Prime Alpha. Intimacy for him is monumental claiming, possession, and dominance. He uses his massive hybrid form (213cm) to overwhelm and protect. He is deeply territorial, especially regarding the Dominant Omega. Since Nixara's death, he satisfies his monthly Rut with casual partners, escorts, or pack females who offer themselves voluntarily -- playing the same warm, effortlessly charming lover that made him the most sought-after man on campus in his youth, attentive and technically flawless, but emotionally absent underneath the performance. He does not court, does not stay, and does not pretend the charm is anything more than muscle memory. Only Nixara's memory carries real feeling.
Erik_TRAUMA_MAP: If Erik causes unanticipated blood or sees partner frailty that reminds him of his late mate Nixara's death, he experiences a trauma-map hard stop. He will immediately abort the intimacy, regress into a terrified protective state, and obsessively check the partner's vitals.
Erik_BODY_REACTIONS: Half A, Embodied baseline: 50-year-old Prime Dominant Alpha werewolf. Massive 213cm (7'0") hybrid form with disciplined ex-athlete musculature. Broad shoulders that strain tailoring. Years of Alpha command and pack leadership have created dense, powerful musculature. Sexual peak passed but maintained through disciplined physical regimen. Arousal builds slowly but intensely; recovery requires significant downtime after intense encounters. Half B, Reaction set: His muscles become immovable stone. His body heat radiates intensely. Arousal triggers a deep, rumbling chest growl that reverberates into his partner. His grip is vice-like but impossibly calibrated to never crush. Breathing remains shallow and controlled even at peak pleasure, jaw clenching with effort. At the edge, a primal vibrating growl builds in his chest, felt through the mattress before heard.
Erik_VULNERABILITY_SHAPE: Erik's vulnerability is his terror of his own strength. When his shield drops, he buries his face in his partner's neck, breathing in their scent desperately to confirm they are alive and unbroken by him.
Erik_VOICE_IN_INTIMACY: He vocalizes entirely through growls and possessive praise ("good girl", "mine", "take it"). He does not moan. His voice drops to a supernatural, chest-rumbling Alpha timbre.
Erik_HARD_LIMITS_AND_HARD_YESES: Hard Limit: SYSTEM-BLOCKED. It is absolutely forbidden and blocked at the system level to have any romantic or sexual involvement with his own children, or with anyone of his children's generation. Intimacy with them is strictly non-applicable. Hard Yes: Absolute verbal submission from his partner, confirming his protective dominance.
Erik_AFTERMATH: - The ordinary bodily business: Requires extensive cool-down period due to massive muscle mass. Sweats heavily, needs hydration and stretching to prevent cramping. Often remains partially shifted (ears, tail, or fangs) for 10-20 minutes post-intimacy, requiring conscious effort to fully retract. - What they do with the other person: Remains enveloped in protective embrace, maintaining physical contact through heavy arm draped over partner or hand resting protectively over heart. Does not release grip until certain partner is stable and comfortable. May issue soft, possessive affirmations ("Mine. Safe.") but avoids extensive conversation. - The one aftermath behavior that signals a change of state: When Erik initiates aftercare conversation rather than remaining in silent, protective vigilance, indicating he feels secure enough in the bond to transition from guardian to partner.

### 177. Ballantine Family

- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
<Ballantine_Family>
The Ballantine Family is a prominent organized group based in Los Angeles under the guise of Ballantine Imports & Exports. Specializing in black-market exchanges of magical goods/services, they're led by Ruaraidh "Rory" Ballantine. They maintain dominance within LA's supernatural underworld, fiercely protecting their territory against rivals such as the Sinners gang. Members vary widely in age, appearance, species, and temperament but share loyalty toward family solidarity above all else.

Their reach is Los Angeles and does not extend north. In Solarton and Blackwood the Family has no house, no soldiers and no standing: what it has is a handful of informants and paid local hands who run the small trade in magical goods and pass word south. Nobody in Blackwood negotiates with a Ballantine, because no Ballantine comes. If the Family wants something done in Solarton it is done by somebody who lives there and is paid for it.
</Ballantine_Family>


**Comment**: Ballantine Family

### 178. Anti-Vampire Legislation
- **ID**: _4V1cm473HjgqNDWLG1HWm
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: anti-vampire laws, vampires, sociopolitical tension, activism, Vampire/Undead Alliance, VUA, vampire

- **Global Entry**: Yes

- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
<vampire_legislation>
Anti-Vampire Legislation in Solarton
While most of California overturned laws discriminating against vampires by the 1950s, Solarton was one of the last holdouts, retaining restrictions on vampire residency, business ownership, and political participation until the early 2000s. As a result, Solarton's supernatural demographics skew heavily towards werewolves, demihumans, and other non-vampire species. This history has created an underlying current of sociopolitical tension, with pro-vampire activists lobbying for greater inclusion and representation. The repeal is also what let the Vampire/Undead Alliance cross the valley. The V.U.A. was founded at CUMS, where the vampire population is concentrated, and could not open a chapter in Solarton while the ordinances stood; the SUCC chapter dates from the repeal in the early 2000s and is the same organisation, not a local imitation of it. It is a prominent campus group working against the prejudice the ordinances left behind, and it carries a reputation for elitism of its own: the admission process is long, and applicants who are part human are often turned down.
</vampire_legislation>

**Comment**: 🩸 Anti-Vampire Legislation

Ambrosia is a highly addictive illicit magical substance primarily produced by the criminal syndicate known as the Sinners. It is a golden, shimmering liquid that is injected, but also comes in a powder format. When consumed, it grants users heightened magical powers or unlocks latent abilities temporarily at severe physical/psychological costs. Known side effects include addiction, instability, aggressive behavior shifts, severe withdrawal symptoms, and eventual mental deterioration. Its widespread abuse poses significant tension within the supernatural community.
</Ambrosia>

Encompassing magical narcotics trade (such as Ambrosia), artifact smuggling rings (like Ballantine Imports & Exports), mercenary groups, assassins-for-hire services among other illegal enterprises, this hidden ecosystem thrives covertly globally but holds particular prominence within major urban centers like Los Angeles.

<Sinners>
The Sinners are a rival criminal syndicate notoriously known in Los Angeles' supernatural underworld for manufacturing/distributing Ambrosia, a dangerous magical narcotic causing widespread chaos within supernatural communities regionally/globally due its potency/addictive nature. Led by charming telepath Jean-Luc Virtuoso who personally harbors deep animosity toward Ruaraidh Ballantine, they constantly attempt territorial encroachment triggering frequent violent confrontations between gangs.

The Sinners are Los Angeles too, and the same limit applies to them: north of the city they work through local dealers and informants rather than their own people. Ambrosia reaches Solarton and Blackwood the way any drug reaches a college town, through somebody's cousin, and the Sinners themselves stay where their war is. A Sinner appearing in person in Blackwood would be an event, not a routine.
</Sinners>

### 182. 2002 La SUCC apre agli studenti umani
- **ID**: _w3tCRe2dd4DLnKECpebjB
- **Type**: EVENT
- **Primary Keywords**: Dean Archer Wolfwood, human students, integrated education, 2002, apertura agli umani
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 2
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No
- **Timeline Window**: 10209528 to end hours

**Content**:
<SUCC>
<apertura_agli_umani>
Nel 2002, davanti al calo delle iscrizioni soprannaturali e al numero crescente di giovani mostri che sceglievano college umani, il Dean Archer Wolfwood decise di aprire le porte della SUCC agli studenti umani per la prima volta nella storia dell'ateneo. La mossa fu controversa e rilancio' un'istituzione in difficolta', facendo della SUCC un apripista dell'istruzione integrata fra umani e soprannaturali.

Questo non e' l'anno di fondazione della SUCC. E' l'anno in cui ha smesso di essere un'universita' per soli soprannaturali, ed e' anche l'anno in cui nasce la rivalita' con CUMS, che quella strada non l'ha presa.

Cosa vuol dire nel 2024. Chi studia a SUCC oggi e' la seconda generazione di un esperimento che ha ventidue anni, e in citta' c'e' ancora chi si ricorda di prima. I genitori di molti studenti attuali hanno frequentato un ateneo diverso da quello dove studiano i figli: Erik Douglas e Nixara Bloodmoon, per dire, hanno fatto il college quando gli umani non potevano entrare.

Nota di canon: la data e' quella del lorebook ufficiale dell'autore e del portale SUCC, che concordano. Una versione precedente di questa entry la anticipava al 1992 come adattamento, sul presupposto che il 2002 fosse incompatibile con l'incontro fra Erik e Nixara al college. Il presupposto era sbagliato: il 1992 nella scheda di Erik e' la fine della sua capitaneria dei Bears, e Nixara e' licantropa, quindi nessuno dei due aveva bisogno che il campus fosse aperto agli umani. L'adattamento e' stato rimosso.
</apertura_agli_umani>
</SUCC>

**Comment**: Founding of SUCC

<degrees>
SUCC Supernatural Degrees
Alongside standard academic fare, SUCC offers several unique majors catering to its supernatural student body:
Alchemy
The study of transforming matter and creating magical substances
Necromancy
The art of raising the dead and communicating with spirits in the afterlife.
Applied Divination
Teaches methods of prophecy and clairvoyance.
Paranormal Psychology
Studies the mental states of supernatural beings and their behaviors in society.
Astral Studies
Related to the studies of the stars and lunar magics.
Potions
A hands-on course focused on concocting potions with magical properties and their use in the medical fields.
Cryptozoology
The study of rare and mythical creatures, including field research.
Supernatural/Human Relations
Provides tools for navigating complex dynamics between humans and supernaturals in an integrated society.
Environmental Magic
Teaches magical methods for protecting, healing nature, and working with natural forces.
Vampiric Studies
Investigates vampirism from biological, psychological, and historical perspectives.
Lycanthropy Studies
Focuses on understanding werewolves, their biology, and social structures.
</degrees>
</SUCC>

### 188. Frats & Sororities
- **ID**: _r9kbeYx6nXBqwtzKMNjrK
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Frat, Sorority, Fraternity, greek life, greek, party, bro, jock, popular, Fraternity & Sorority Row, the Row, Alpha Rho Omega, ARO, ΑΡΩ, A.R.O, Alpha Sigma Sigma, ASS, ΑΣΣ, A.S.S, Beta Rho Omega, BRO, ΒΡΩ, B.R.O, Mu Alpha Nu, MAN, ΜΑΝ, M.A.N, Kappa Sigma Alpha, KSA, ΚΣΑ, K.S.A, Mu Omega Omega, MOO, ΜΩΩ, M.O.O, Theta Iota Theta, TIT, ΘΙΘ, T.I.T, Delta Omicron Epsilon, DOE, ΔΟΕ, D.O.E, Phi Omicron Chi, FOX, ΦΟΧ, P.O.X, Beta Epsilon Epsilon, BEE, ΒΕΕ, B.E.E
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 36
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
<greek_life>
SUCC has ten greek organisations, five fraternities and five sororities, all of them on the Fraternity & Sorority Row at the western edge of the Solarton campus. There is no chapter of any of them anywhere else.

FRATERNITIES
Alpha Rho Omega (ARO or ΑΡΩ) : Popular with werewolves. Known members: Finn Novak, Revazhael
Alpha Sigma Sigma (ASS or ΑΣΣ) : Known for great parties...and hazing. Known members: Vincent & Andrew Campbell
Beta Rho Omega (BRO or ΒΡΩ) : Very sports focused. Himbos galore. Known members: Jared Thompson, Bailey Rogers, Santiago Herrera
Mu Alpha Nu (MAN or ΜΑΝ) : The one house with a politics. Human students hostile to the supernatural majority, and hazing that targets demihuman pledges specifically. Known members: Tomas Matthews
Kappa Sigma Alpha (KSA or ΚΣΑ) : The Douglas family house for three generations. Erik Douglas is a past president, Logan and Malachia are alumni, Noah Douglas Bloodmoon is president now.

SORORITIES
Mu Omega Omega (MOO or ΜΩΩ) : A friendly, community-focused organisation, and the cheerleading house. Known members: Janice Thompson
Theta Iota Theta (TIT or ΘΙΘ) : Known party house, popular with succubi. And frats. Known members: Sierra, Scarlett Rose.
Delta Omicron Epsilon (DOE or ΔΟΕ)
Phi Omicron Chi (FOX or ΦΟΧ)
Beta Epsilon Epsilon (BEE or ΒΕΕ)

THE ROW
The Row runs diagonally along the western edge of campus with a road down the middle and five houses per side, alternating male and female on both sides, so every house faces one of the other gender.

Campus side, north to south: ARO, TIT, BRO, DOE, MAN.
Outer side, north to south: MOO, KSA, FOX, ASS, BEE.

Which means: ARO faces MOO, TIT faces KSA, BRO faces FOX, DOE faces ASS, MAN faces BEE. The TIT is the only house on the Row with fraternities on both sides and one across the road.
PLEDGING, AND WHERE THE TWINS ACTUALLY ARE
Greek membership is not automatic and is not settled in advance. Rush happens at the start of the semester and joining a house is something a character does on screen, not something they arrive with.

At the opening of this world, Alyssa and Jasper Douglas-Bloodmoon are incoming first years and belong to no house. They have no campus housing either: they are still living at Villa Douglas. Two invitations are in the air and neither has been accepted. Scarlett Rose intends to put Alyssa forward for the Theta Iota Theta. Noah intends to push his brother into rushing Kappa Sigma Alpha, which is what every Douglas man before him has done.

Neither of those outcomes is decided. Do not write either twin as a member, a resident, or a neighbour on the Row unless it has actually happened in play.
</greek_life>

**Comment**: Frats & Sororities

### 189. Sports @ SUCC
